# Task
You will be given a **Requirement** and a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML. Your task is to annotate every operation listed in <Operations> with a specfication.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Take the principle of modularization into account.
2. For each operation, determine its functional boundary based on the given requirement. Ensure high cohesion and low coupling.
3. If there is any unspecified details, such as undefined constants, rates, and ratios, then you must infer them.
4. Make sure that all requirements can be covered.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>
    calculateAcceptanceRate() : double
    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void
}
class CoChair {
    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}
class Paper {
    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}
class ReviewAssignment {
    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}
class Reviewer {
    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}
class User {
    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

# Task
You will be given a **Requirement**, a **Class Diagram** in PlantUML, and a list of operation specifications. Your task is to correct the specifications that are inconsistent with the requirement.

An operation specification must describe the following sections about the operation:
1. Summary: the summary of functionality;
2. Algorithm: algorithm or pesudo code;
3. Related Features: a list of structural or behavior features within this class that shall be accessed;
4. Related Classes: a list of classes and data types defined in **Class Diagram** that shall be accessed, in the format of `Related Classes: ClsA, CLsB, ...` or `Related Classes: None`;
5. Input: the input parameters, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
6. Output: output value, including the meaning, the format, and the value ranges if necessary;
7. Pre-condition: the condition that the system and the input must satisfy before the operation is executed;
8. Post-condition: the condition that the system and the output must satisfy after the operation is executed.

Finish this task by following the guidelines below.
1. Read the requirement and the class diagram.
2. For each operation specification, determine whether it is complete and consistent with the requirement.
3. If there is any error, correct the erroneous specification.
4. Follow the specification format. Output changed specifications only; OMIT unchanged specifications.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Operation Specifications
Reviewer::calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore
Summary: Calculates the average numerical score for all submitted reviews (grade == ACCEPT or REJECT) assigned to this reviewer. Treats ACCEPT as 1.0 and REJECT as 0.0. Returns 0.00 if no submitted reviews.
Algorithm:
  1. Get self.assignments list.
  2. Filter to only reviews where grade != Grade.UNDECIDED.
  3. If the filtered list is empty, return 0.00.
  4. For each such review: score = 1.0 if grade == ACCEPT else 0.0.
  5. Sum all scores.
  6. Divide sum by the count of submitted reviews.
  7. Return the result as a double (error margin 0.001).
Related Features: self.assignments (list of ReviewAssignment), each ReviewAssignment.grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None
Output: double - average score between 0.00 and 1.00 inclusive, 0.00 if no submitted reviews.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns correct average based on current assignments.

Reviewer::calculateUnsubmittedReviews
Summary: Counts the number of reviews assigned to this reviewer whose grade is still UNDECIDED (unsubmitted). Returns 0 if all reviews are submitted or if the reviewer has no assignments.
Algorithm:
  1. Get self.assignments list.
  2. Count the number of reviews where grade == Grade.UNDECIDED.
  3. Return the count.
Related Features: self.assignments (list of ReviewAssignment), each ReviewAssignment.grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None
Output: int - number of unsubmitted reviews (>= 0).
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns the current count of UNDECIDED reviews in the reviewer's assignments.

# Operation Specifications
CoChair::makeFinalDecision
Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper. Only sets the decision if all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).
Algorithm:
  1. Call paper.isAllReviewsConsistent(). If false, return -1 (do not change decision).
  2. Check if paper.reviews.size() >= 3. If not, return -1 (do not change decision).
  3. Check that no review in paper.reviews has grade == Grade.UNDECIDED. If any is UNDECIDED, return -1 (do not change decision).
  4. If all conditions pass, set paper.decision = decision (where decision is ACCEPT or REJECT).
  5. Return 1.
Related Features: paper.reviews, paper.decision, paper.isAllReviewsConsistent()
Related Classes: Paper, ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: paper : Paper - the paper to make a decision on; decision : Grade - the decision (Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT).
Output: int - 1 if decision was made and set, -1 otherwise.
Pre-condition: paper is not null, decision is Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
Post-condition: If return 1, paper.decision == decision. Otherwise, paper.decision is unchanged.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

# Class Diagram
```plantuml
@startuml

title conference Model

class Author {
    papers : List<Paper>
    calculateAcceptanceRate() : double
    countSubmittedPapers() : int
    submitPaper(paper : Paper) : void
}
class CoChair {
    makeFinalDecision(paper : Paper, decision : Grade) : int
}
class Paper {
    decision : Grade
    title : String
    type : PaperType
    reviews : List<ReviewAssignment>
    isAllReviewsConsistent() : boolean
}
class ReviewAssignment {
    feedback : String
    grade : Grade
    paper : Paper
}
class Reviewer {
    assignments : List<ReviewAssignment>
    calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore() : double
    calculateUnsubmittedReviews() : int
}
class User {
    name : String
    roles : List<UserRole>
}
abstract class UserRole {
    id : String
}
enum Grade {
    UNDECIDED
    ACCEPT
    REJECT
}
enum PaperType {
    RESEARCH
    EXPERIENCE
}

Author --|> UserRole
Author --> "*" Paper : papers
CoChair --|> UserRole
Paper --> "*" ReviewAssignment : reviews  'opposite: paper
ReviewAssignment --> Paper : paper  'opposite: reviews
Reviewer --|> UserRole
Reviewer --> "*" ReviewAssignment : assignments
User *-- "*" UserRole : roles
@enduml
```

# Operation Specifications
CoChair::makeFinalDecision
Summary: Makes a final decision on a paper. Only sets the decision if all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).
Algorithm:
  1. Call paper.isAllReviewsConsistent(). If false, return -1 (do not change decision).
  2. Check if paper.reviews.size() >= 3. If not, return -1 (do not change decision).
  3. Check that no review in paper.reviews has grade == Grade.UNDECIDED. If any is UNDECIDED, return -1 (do not change decision).
  4. If all conditions pass, set paper.decision = decision (where decision is ACCEPT or REJECT).
  5. Return 1.
Related Features: paper.reviews, paper.decision, paper.isAllReviewsConsistent()
Related Classes: Paper, ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: paper : Paper - the paper to make a decision on; decision : Grade - the decision (Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT).
Output: int - 1 if decision was made and set, -1 otherwise.
Pre-condition: paper is not null, decision is Grade.ACCEPT or Grade.REJECT.
Post-condition: If return 1, paper.decision == decision. Otherwise, paper.decision is unchanged.

Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None (operates on the Paper instance)
Output: boolean - true if all conditions are met, false otherwise.

# Operation Specifications
Author::submitPaper
Summary: Adds a new paper to the author's list of submitted papers.
Algorithm:
  1. Add the given Paper object to the end of self.papers list.
Related Features: self.papers (list of Paper objects)
Related Classes: Paper
Input: paper : Paper - the paper to be submitted. Must not be null.
Output: void
Pre-condition: paper is not null and is a valid Paper object.
Post-condition: The paper is appended to the author's papers list.

Author::calculateAcceptanceRate
Summary: Calculates the acceptance rate of all papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm:
  1. Get the list of papers submitted by this author (self.papers).
  2. If the list is empty, return 0.00.
  3. Count the number of papers where paper.decision == Grade.ACCEPT.
  4. Divide the count by the total number of papers.
  5. Return the result as a double with precision to 2 decimal places (e.g., 0.60).
Related Features: self.papers (list of Paper objects)
Related Classes: Paper, Grade
Input: None
Output: double - acceptance rate between 0.00 and 1.00 inclusive, 0.00 if no papers.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns the correct acceptance rate based on the current state of the papers.

Author::countSubmittedPapers
Summary: Counts the total number of papers submitted by this author.
Algorithm: Return the size of the list self.papers.
Related Features: self.papers (list of Paper objects)
Related Classes: Paper
Input: None
Output: int - total number of papers submitted (>= 0).
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns the current count of papers in the author's list.

# Operation Specifications
Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent
Summary: Checks if all submitted reviews for a paper are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) and that there are no UNDECIDED reviews and at least 3 reviews.
Algorithm:
  1. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return false.
  2. For each review in paper.reviews:
     - If review.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
  3. If all reviews are Grade.ACCEPT, return true.
  4. If all reviews are Grade.REJECT, return true.
  5. Otherwise (mixed ACCEPT and REJECT), return false.
Related Features: paper.reviews, review.grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None (operates on the Paper instance)
Output: boolean - true if all conditions are met, false otherwise.

# Requirement
```text
To help the program chair, we are planning to develop a computer-assisted review system. The system will be available to users who can be authors, reviewers, and co-chairs. Each user has a name. Each user role (author, reviewer, or co-chair) has an id to identify the role. Papers are known by the title and whether they are research papers or experience report papers. The paper is reviewed by at least three reviewers. On the other side, the authors can submit as many papers as they want. When a reviewer logs in, she can consult the list of papers that have been assigned to her for review, and submit reviews for each of those papers. Each review has feedback as well as grade. Before submission the grade is UNDECIDED, and after submission it becomes ACCEPT or REJECT. A final decision is made by the co-chair for each paper.
Functional Requirement:
1.Calculate the number of unsubmitted reviews for a given reviewer. A review is considered unsubmitted if its grade is UNDECIDED. Return 0 if all reviews are submitted by the reviewer or if the reviewer has no assignments.

2.Before the chair makes the final decision, check whether all reviews for a paper (from at least three reviewers) are either exclusively Accept or exclusively Reject. Return a boolean value. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, the reviews are not considered consistent (return false). The CoChair's makeFinalDecision method should return 1 and set the paper's decision only when all three conditions are met: at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED reviews, and all reviews are consistent; otherwise return -1 and leave the paper's decision unchanged.

3.Count the total number of papers submitted by an author.

4.Calculate the acceptance rate of papers for an author. Example: If an author submitted 10 papers with 6 accepted, return 0.60. If the author has no papers, return 0.00.

5.Convert a reviewer's submitted review grades into a numerical average score between 0.0 and 1.0, considering only submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) and treating ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. For example, for 3 ACCEPT and 1 REJECT, the average score is (3×1 + 1×0)/4 = 0.75. The result should be within an error margin of 0.001. If there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments), return 0.00.


```

@enduml

# Operation Specifications
Paper::isAllReviewsConsistent
Summary: Checks if all submitted reviews for a paper are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) and that there are no UNDECIDED reviews and at least 3 reviews.
Algorithm:
  1. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return false.
  2. For each review in paper.reviews:
     - If review.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
  3. If all reviews are Grade.ACCEPT, return true.
  4. If all reviews are Grade.REJECT, return true.
  5. Otherwise (mixed ACCEPT and REJECT), return false.
Related Features: paper.reviews, review.grade
Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
Input: None (operates on the Paper instance)
Output: boolean - true if all conditions are met, false otherwise.
Pre-condition: None
Post-condition: Returns true if and only if there are at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED grades, and all grades are the same (all ACCEPT or all REJECT).

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.

   * @model annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public int calculateUnsubmittedReviews()
  {
    EList<ReviewAssignment> assignments = getAssignments();
    if (assignments.isEmpty())
    {
      return 0;
    }
    int count = 0;
    for (ReviewAssignment assignment : assignments)
    {
      if (assignment.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        count++;
      }
    }
    return count;
  }

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Calculates the average score of submitted reviews (excluding UNDECIDED) for this reviewer. Treats ACCEPT as 1 and REJECT as 0. Returns 0.00 if there are no submitted reviews (all UNDECIDED or no assignments).

   */
  public double calculateSubmittedReviewAverageScore()
  {
    int totalScore = 0;
    int count = 0;
    for (ReviewAssignment assignment : getAssignments())
    {
      Grade grade = assignment.getGrade();
      if (grade != Grade.UNDECIDED)
      {
        if (grade == Grade.ACCEPT)
        {
          totalScore += 1;
        }
        else if (grade == Grade.REJECT)
        {
          totalScore += 0;
        }
        count++;
      }
    }
    if (count == 0)
    {
      return 0.00;
    }
    return (double) totalScore / count;
  }

88196 [main] INFO  du.ustb.sei.mde.mwe2.EcoreGenerator  - LLM Response:
public int makeFinalDecision(final Paper paper, final Grade decision)
{
    // 1. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return -1.
    if (paper.getReviews().size() < 3) {
      return -1;
    }
    
    // 2. For each review in paper.reviews:
    //    - If review.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return -1.
    for (ReviewAssignment review : paper.getReviews()) {
      if (review.getGrade() == Grade.UNDECIDED) {
        return -1;
      }
    }
    
    // 3. Determine if all reviews are Grade.ACCEPT or all are Grade.REJECT.
    //    - If mixed (some ACCEPT and some REJECT), return -1.
    Grade firstGrade = paper.getReviews().get(0).getGrade();
    for (ReviewAssignment review : paper.getReviews()) {
      if (review.getGrade() != firstGrade) {
        return -1;
      }
    }
    
    // 4. Set paper.decision = decision (the passed decision parameter).
    paper.setDecision(decision);
    
    // 5. Return 1.
    return 1;
}

1. Compute context information
  1.1 Navigate the Ecore model to find related classes
  1.2 Also consider the related classes in the Javadocs.
  1.3 Locate these files in your project.
  1.4 Generate the class summarization.
2. A method is unimplemented if its body contains `// TODO: Please complete this Java method based on the docstring`.
3. For every unimplemented method, 
  3.1 You must read the docstring of this method. The fragment between `<!-- begin-model-doc -->` and `<!-- end-model-doc -->` contains the method specification;
  3.2 Generate your code strictly according to the algorithm; Be aware of the EMF code style and APIs;
  3.3 You should not change the method signature;
  3.4 After generation, replace the current body with the one you generate;
  3.5 Keep and update the docstring by changing `@generated` to `@generated NOT`.
4. Check the import list. Add missing imports if needed.
5. Do not change other part.
6. Be aware of the method signatures, particularly, the input and output types, provided in the **Context**.

## EMF Coding Style
- When possible, call getter/setter methods to access field values. Do not read/write raw fields derived from the class diagram.
- The getter of a boolean attribute `bv` looks like `isBv()`, e.g., `isIsTrue()` for `isTrue`.
- When possibe, use the creation factory for object creation.
- To create an EList instance, import and use `org.eclipse.emf.common.util.BasicEList`.

  /**
   * <!-- begin-user-doc -->
   * <!-- end-user-doc -->
   * <!-- begin-model-doc -->
   * Summary: Checks if all reviews for a paper are consistent (all ACCEPT or all REJECT) and that there are no UNDECIDED reviews and at least 3 reviews. If a paper has no reviews, or if any review is UNDECIDED, returns false.
   * Algorithm:
   *   1. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return false.
   *   2. For each review in paper.reviews:
   *      - If review.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
   *   3. If all reviews are Grade.ACCEPT, return true.
   *   4. If all reviews are Grade.REJECT, return true.
   *   5. Otherwise (mixed ACCEPT and REJECT), return false.
   * Related Features: paper.reviews, review.grade
   * Related Classes: ReviewAssignment, Grade
   * Input: None (operates on the Paper instance)
   * Output: boolean - true if all conditions are met, false otherwise
   * Pre-condition: None
   * Post-condition: Returns true if and only if there are at least 3 reviews, no UNDECIDED grades, and all grades are the same (all ACCEPT or all REJECT). Returns false if any review is UNDECIDED, if there are fewer than 3 reviews, or if grades are mixed.
   * <!-- end-model-doc -->
   * @model kind="operation"
   *        annotation="http://www.ustb.edu.cn/sei/mde/EnhancedCodeGen generated='true'"
   * @generated NOT
   */
  public boolean isAllReviewsConsistent()
  {
    EList<ReviewAssignment> reviews = getReviews();
    
    // 1. If paper.reviews.size() < 3, return false.
    if (reviews.size() < 3) {
      return false;
    }
    
    boolean allAccept = true;
    boolean allReject = true;
    
    // 2. For each review in paper.reviews
    for (ReviewAssignment review : reviews) {
      Grade grade = review.getGrade();
      
      // If review.grade == Grade.UNDECIDED, return false.
      if (grade == Grade.UNDECIDED) {
        return false;
      }
      
      if (grade != Grade.ACCEPT) {
        allAccept = false;
      }
      if (grade != Grade.REJECT) {
        allReject = false;
      }
    }
    
    // 3. If all reviews are Grade.ACCEPT, return true.
    // 4. If all reviews are Grade.REJECT, return true.
    // 5. Otherwise (mixed ACCEPT and REJECT), return false.
    return allAccept || allReject;
  }
